\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 11. Voice / Singing Live-Output QA Status\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 True Voice / Singing live outputs are not available in the supplied materials. The user explicitly states that there are no voice-only outputs currently available and that Voice / Singing is a new feature being prepared for production release. MT acting+song PDFs and comparison/result examples were supplied, but they are bridge/current-output examples rather than revised Voice-only QA.\
Voice / Singing live QA has therefore not been completed. Untested areas include live Song / Voice report quality, frontend category labels, comparison-page parity, PDF/export behaviour, rendered timestamp parity, persisted-versus-rendered timestamp parity, score stability, and behaviour after implementation/rollout.\
Live materials required later include at minimum: Song-only report with brief, Song-only report with no brief, poor-audio/track-balance Song report, simple home-capture Song report, polished-production Song report, MT acting+song report, rendered category-label screenshot, comparison-page screenshot/export, timestamped-notes section, next-take plan, presentation/professional standards section, JSON/report object snippet, PDF/export if available, access/adapted-context output if available, and repeated same-video outputs for score stability.\
Absence of live outputs does not block research maturity. It does block product release sign-off for implemented revised Voice / Singing behaviour.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 12. Voice / Singing Patch Decision\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\fs24 \cf0 No Voice / Singing patch required; branch is mature enough for implementation planning with limitations.
\f1\b0 \
Rationale: Voice / Singing meets the lean v5 standard at research/rubric/output-specificity level. It has preflight, source census, targeted extraction batches, synthesis, audit, revision, final audit and output-specificity mapping. It preserves the live TapeCoach architecture, clarifies the overloaded 
\f2\fs26 vocal
\f1\fs24  field for Song / MT, protects Acting and Dance label containment, provides an observable descriptor spine, separates interpretation from acting-scene leakage, establishes no-diagnosis vocal-health rules, codifies access-safe inclusive voice handling, and creates output-specificity tests and live-QA requirements. Residual weaknesses are correctly carried as limitations rather than hidden or over-implemented.\
Critical blockers: none for implementation planning preparation.\
Patch scope if needed: none. The remaining work is live-output QA after implementation or current-output bridge checks where relevant, not a VOICE-PATCH.\
What should not be reopened: VOICE-S0 source census, B1\'96B4 extraction, VOICE-SYN, VOICE-AUDIT, VOICE-REV, VOICE-FINAL-AUDIT, or VOICE-OUTPUT-SPEC.\
Deep Research required: no.\
Standard ChatGPT sufficient: yes, for any future implementation-readiness handoff consolidation or live-QA prompt creation.\
Live outputs required for patch: no, because no patch is recommended.\
Can proceed before implementation planning: yes, with explicit limitations carried into implementation planning and later live-output QA.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 13. If Patch Is Recommended: VOICE-PATCH Scope\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 No VOICE-PATCH scope required.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 14. Reusable Handoff Pack\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 VOICE-V5-MATURITY-AUDIT reviewed the completed Voice / Singing branch against the newer lean v5 branch-development standard. The maturity decision is that no Voice / Singing patch is required. The branch is mature enough for future implementation planning with explicit limitations and later live-output QA.\
The strongest Voice / Singing assets are its full traceability spine and mature safety/fairness boundaries. The branch preserves VOICE-S001\'96VOICE-S052, VOICE-SYN-F01\'96F17, VOICE-AUDIT-I01\'96I20, VOICE-AUDIT-R01\'96R21, VOICE-REV-C01\'96C17, VOICE-REV-S01\'96S18, VOICE-FINAL-D01, VOICE-OS-R onward, VOICE-OS-T01\'96T31 and VOICE-OS-I01\'96I12. It clarifies 
\f2\fs26 vocal
\f1\fs24  as sung-vocal evidence in Song / Musical Theatre while preserving Acting speech-delivery and Dance no-singing containment. It keeps Song vocal-centred but requires interpretive evidence where assessable: lyric intention, communication, phrase action, story and acting-through-song. It converts formal frameworks into observable descriptor language for pitch, rhythm, tone, diction, phrasing, communication and interpretation without importing marks, grades or weights.\
The branch also strongly separates audio/technical assessability from vocal merit, treats accompaniment/backing/self-accompaniment as context-specific, blocks production polish and paid-resource access as talent evidence, and protects fixed-material policy. Safety and fairness coverage is mature: no vocal-health diagnosis from tape; strain-like, fatigued or hoarse sound remains observation-only; access needs, disability, neurodivergence, hearing difference, accent, speech difference, gender-diverse voice and assistive technology are non-deficit context.\
Limitations remain but are correctly carried forward: detailed belt/mix/registration evidence is thin; jazz/folk/broad commercial-pop descriptors are uneven; full actor-musician evidence is partial; neurodivergence operational detail is broad; no true Voice-only live outputs exist; frontend labels, comparison-page parity, rendered timestamp parity, score stability, implementation behaviour and rollout effects remain unverified.\
The branch does not need a focused patch. Later work should be implementation planning plus live-output QA when Voice / Singing outputs exist. Live-output QA is not required to prove research maturity, but it is required before release confidence for implemented revised behaviour.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0

\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0
